00025: def B(a,k,s,p,c):
00026:     return a.include(k,box(size=s,center=p,color=c))
00027: def R(a,k,p,q,r,c):
00028:     return a.include(k,rod(radius=r,start=p,end=q,color=c))
00029: def T(a,k,pts,r,c):
00030:     return a.include(k,sweep_tube(points=pts,radius=r,color=c))
00031: def ring(a,k,p,r,t,c,plane="xz"):
00032:     pts=[]
00033:     for j in range(25):
00034:         v=j*math.tau/24
00035:         if plane=="xz":
00036:             pts.append((p[0]+r*math.cos(v),p[1],p[2]+r*math.sin(v)))
00037:         elif plane=="xy":
00038:             pts.append((p[0]+r*math.cos(v),p[1]+r*math.sin(v),p[2]))
00039:         else:
00040:             pts.append((p[0],p[1]+r*math.cos(v),p[2]+r*math.sin(v)))
00041:     return T(a,k,pts,t,c)

00023: def B(a,k,s,p,c):
00024:     return a.include(k,box(size=s,center=p,color=c))
00025: def R(a,k,p,q,r,c):
00026:     return a.include(k,rod(radius=r,start=p,end=q,color=c))
00027: def T(a,k,pts,r,c):
00028:     return a.include(k,sweep_tube(points=pts,radius=r,color=c))
00029: def ring(a,k,p,r,t,c,plane="xz"):
00030:     pts=[]
00031:     for j in range(25):
00032:         v=j*math.tau/24
00033:         if plane=="xz":
00034:             pts.append((p[0]+r*math.cos(v),p[1],p[2]+r*math.sin(v)))
00035:         elif plane=="xy":
00036:             pts.append((p[0]+r*math.cos(v),p[1]+r*math.sin(v),p[2]))
00037:         else:
00038:             pts.append((p[0],p[1]+r*math.cos(v),p[2]+r*math.sin(v)))
00039:     return T(a,k,pts,t,c)

00042: def detail(group,size,center,color,angle=0):
00043:     key=(group,tuple(color))
00044:     verts,faces=details.setdefault(key,([],[]))
00045:     x,y,z=center
00046:     sx,sy,sz=[v/2 for v in size]
00047:     c,s=math.cos(angle),math.sin(angle)
00048:     n=len(verts)
00049:     for dx,dy,dz in [(-sx,-sy,-sz),(sx,-sy,-sz),(sx,sy,-sz),(-sx,sy,-sz),
00050:                      (-sx,-sy,sz),(sx,-sy,sz),(sx,sy,sz),(-sx,sy,sz)]:
00051:         verts.append((x+c*dx-s*dy,y+s*dx+c*dy,z+dz))
00052:     faces.extend(tuple(n+i for i in f) for f in
00053:                  [(0,3,2,1),(4,5,6,7),(0,1,5,4),
00054:                   (1,2,6,5),(2,3,7,6),(3,0,4,7)])

00042: def detail(group,size,center,color,angle=0):
00043:     # Collect disconnected cuboids by semantic group and color.
00044:     key=(group,tuple(color))
00045:     verts,faces=details.setdefault(key,([],[]))
00046:     x,y,z=center
00047:     sx,sy,sz=[v/2 for v in size]
00048:     c,s=math.cos(angle),math.sin(angle)
00049:     n=len(verts)
00050:     for dx,dy,dz in [(-sx,-sy,-sz),(sx,-sy,-sz),(sx,sy,-sz),(-sx,sy,-sz),
00051:                      (-sx,-sy,sz),(sx,-sy,sz),(sx,sy,sz),(-sx,sy,sz)]:
00052:         verts.append((x+c*dx-s*dy,y+s*dx+c*dy,z+dz))

00036: def boxpart(a,key,size,center,color):

00040:     return a.include(key,rod(radius=r,start=p,end=q,color=color))
00041: 
00042: def detail(group,size,center,color,angle=0):
00043:     key=(group,tuple(color))
00044:     verts,faces=details.setdefault(key,([],[]))
00045:     x,y,z=center
00046:     sx,sy,sz=[v/2 for v in size]
00047:     c,s=math.cos(angle),math.sin(angle)
00048:     n=len(verts)
00049:     for dx,dy,dz in [(-sx,-sy,-sz),(sx,-sy,-sz),(sx,sy,-sz),(-sx,sy,-sz),
00050:                      (-sx,-sy,sz),(sx,-sy,sz),(sx,sy,sz),(-sx,sy,sz)]:
00051:         verts.append((x+c*dx-s*dy,y+s*dx+c*dy,z+dz))
00052:     faces.extend(tuple(n+i for i in f) for f in
00053:                  [(0,3,2,1),(4,5,6,7),(0,1,5,4),
00054:                   (1,2,6,5),(2,3,7,6),(3,0,4,7)])
00055: 
00056: def wire(group,points,r,color):
00057:     curves.append((group,points,r,tuple(color)))
00058: 
00059: def loop(group,center,rx,ry,color,r=.003,phase=0):
00060:     x,y,z=center
00061:     pts=[]
00062:     for j in range(33):
00063:         t=phase+2*math.pi*j/32
00064:         pts.append((x+rx*math.cos(t),y+ry*math.sin(t),
00065:                     z+.002*math.sin(3*t)))
00066:     wire(group,pts,r,color)
